The title, when and why it was asked, then a drawn overview whose form follows the question: **side by side** for a comparison (two columns, up to four rows, an "In short" line), **steps** for a process (numbered), or up to **three key points** for an idea; Helm's own components, never a generated image, so it reads in dark, at 320 and to a screen reader. Then "Videos · 2" at most, as `LinkPreviewCard`s with a play mark and length, opening in a new tab (left out when none is good enough). The caution line "From general knowledge and the web, not checked against your records." Then "Save to my knowledge" (K5, K6) · "Done with it" (off the list, Undo).
- **Research before a dated project** (RA1, RA2). When a dated project nears and something in it needs finding out first (the move's apartments), {Name} offers once, on the project's page, as a dashed card: "Want me to look into apartments before you start touring?" with its reasons ("Your lease ends Sun, Feb 28, 15 weeks from now." · "Nothing in Move yet says what you're looking for.") and "Not now" · "Ask me a few things first". The questions come **before** it looks, in one `FormSheet` "Before I look into apartments", each answer already filled from the person's records where it can be and saying where from (Where: Ballard and Fremont, "From what you said · Nov 10"; Must have: "Pets: a cat and a dog", hollow ring "{Name} inferred · Miso and Pepper"; Move in by: "From Move's target date"); Rent a month takes one amount or a range. "Look into it" sends it: "Looking into apartments: 2 bedrooms in Ballard or Fremont, $2,200 to $2,800, pets allowed. Ready by morning. · Cancel" (: an offer taken up weeks ahead is the one case with a promised time; see Research has a depth). It comes back as a topic in Knowledge ("📦 Move › Apartments"), the brief at its top, every part marked "From the web" with its links, and told once on the project's page ("Looked into apartments in Ballard and Fremont for you. · Open it"). Research reads the web; it never contacts anyone or fills a form. No separate apartments app is assumed.

**Hands-free, and how {Name} knows you.** The owner: assume the person's hands are busy; reading what they say is how {Name} learns them; they see and correct what it believes. Drawn on row 20 (SY1, PT1, HV1, AN1, WS1, LC1, LC2, MF1) and as a listening strip on every question in rows 13 to 19.

- **Every question can be answered by voice.** A reply that asks something back is spoken (when the person came by voice, or hands-free is on), then {Name} listens for 8 seconds with no tap. Under its choices the `ListeningStrip`: `Ear`, "Listening · say “…”" with at most three phrases, a draining bar; after the window, "Say “Hey {Name}” to answer". "Hey {Name}" while Helm is open answers the question still on screen for up to 2 minutes, then starts a new turn; a message typed in between doesn't close the question (, 1c). Speaking over a reply stops it at once, and what was cut off stays (V5). Taps always work and are never required. Free answers are understood ("yeah let's do it in person, maybe at their place"), not only the listed phrases. Round 6's "Answer without tapping" switch becomes **on by default**.
- **Act now, or "Noted"** (AN1). {Name} always answers what was said in a line that addresses it, then acts or says it's noted. It acts at once when the person asked for something, when it changes today or the next 48 hours, or when it is about health or care ("I'm out of Vitamin D" → a task, Undo). Otherwise: "That sounds like a good way to end the week. Noted." with a quiet `BookmarkCheck` line "Noted · {Name} will think it over tonight" and "Forget it". Never silent.
- **How {Name} sees you** (SY1). P5 becomes the place where the person sees how the system reads them, in four groups (Tendencies, How you talk, What works for you, Habits) above Who you are. Every line says where it came from (what you said, quoted on open; ticks over so many weeks; emails); a filled ring is said, hollow is noticed. A line opens to its sources and two actions: "That's not quite right" (say or type how it is; the correction becomes a you-said line that outranks the guess) and "Remove this" ({Name} won't draw it from those words again; the words themselves stay). Both work by voice from anywhere ("no, guitar's fine on weekends"), told with Undo. Health and care appear only as what happened, never as a judgement.
- **How {Name} talks** (PT1). Settings → Your assistant → How {Name} talks: up to 5 traits (tech's closed list: Honest, Brief, Detailed, Warm, Dry, Blunt, Unvarnished, Gentle, Playful, Casual, Formal, Encouraging), how honest on a 3-notch dial (Softens hard news · Says it plainly · Holds nothing back), and a sample reply that changes as they pick. A clash from a fixed list (Blunt–Gentle, Brief–Detailed, Playful–Formal, Casual–Formal, Dry–Warm, Unvarnished–Gentle) is warned in an `InlineNotice` with what {Name} will do ("say what's true plainly and keep the wording soft") and "Keep both" · "Drop one"; it never blocks. Said in a turn ("be more blunt with me") with Undo. Replaces the single Tone; Composed carries over as Honest, Brief, Warm. Health and care stay careful whatever is picked; no trait permits made-up facts.
- **Signals both ways** (WS1, LN7). A fixed list, counted only when said about one habit: hard going (tired, reluctant, too busy, unwell or in pain, bored, stressed) raises LN7; going well (enjoyed, easy, proud, energised, made progress) raises "Going well", 3 in 2 weeks, ask-first, with the back-off: "Weekend mornings seem to suit guitar. Keep them?" · "Keep weekend mornings" · "Make it a bit longer" (only while the habit keeps 4 of 5) · Not now. A solid `accent` card, not dashed: it is about something already theirs. Never on health or care.
- **Where people are** (LC1, LC2). You and every who have one home and any number of dated times away, set from what's said ("I'm in Leavenworth till Tuesday"), trips (Waypoint), the calendar, or the Where card ("Add time away", a `FormSheet` with `CitySelect` and `DateRangeField`). "Nearby" and suggested times use them. This phone's location is the person's choice of **Off** (default) · **City only** · **Precise**, read only while Helm is open. It is never asked up front: when a request would be answered better with a finer setting than the person allows, or they ask for it, {Name}'s answer ends with one `AbilityOfferRow` ("Use this phone's location · Precise", a muted line on what it keeps, Not now · Turn on); Turn on starts the phone's own permission inside the tap. Not now hides it for 4 weeks unless asked again; a phone that said no shows how to allow it in the phone's settings instead.
- **Away in another time zone** (SA4). Reminders, the day's turnover, planning and scheduled actions run on home time. When a time away the person states (said, or added on the Where card), or a Waypoint trip, is in another time zone, the reply that sets it ends with one `AbilityOfferRow`, and nothing else changes: not a travel mode, only that span. `Globe` "Use Lisbon time · Dec 2 – Dec 9", keeps line "Lisbon is 8 hours ahead. Reminders, when your day starts and what {Name} plans follow Lisbon, then go back to Seattle time on Thu, Dec 10.", Not now · Use Lisbon time. When a scheduled action has a run inside the span, the same card adds a second line under a hairline: `Pause` "Pause what runs at home" · "Sunday grocery list, Sun, Dec 6" with "Pause it". Unlike the location offer, voice works ("Lisbon time", "pause it", "both"), since no browser permission is asked. Each folds once taken ("On Lisbon time Dec 2 – Dec 9 · Undo"; "Paused Sunday grocery list for Sun, Dec 6 · Undo"). Not now keeps home time for that span and is never offered again for it. The Where card's row then reads "Away · Lisbon · Dec 2 – Dec 9 · on Lisbon time"; changing the span's dates moves the time with it, and removing the span puts everything back.
- **How a meeting meets** (MF1). Helm's own events carry how (In person · Video · Phone) and where, set from what's said or "Change how it meets"; a Google event's own video link or place is read and shown "From Google Calendar", and what the person says about it is kept beside it, never written to Google.
- **Nothing learned is kept on one phone.** Patterns, observations, How {Name} sees you, traits and locations are sealed records that sync; a new phone shows the same page.
- **Research has a depth, and depth is how widely it reads, never when it arrives** (RA2). **Quick look** (the default) reads a few good sources for a few good options; **Thorough** reads more widely (more listings, guides and reviews) and counts toward a monthly allowance, "about 4 thorough looks left this month" (a $2 monthly allowance per person; quick looks draw on it too), shown only on the brief and in the question. {Name} asks "Quick look or thorough?" only when the research feeds a decision with money or a date in it, or the question is wide open, and says which it would pick. **When it arrives is one rule:** anything asked for in a turn starts at once, a quick look in a few minutes and a thorough one in about 5 to 10 minutes ("Looking into it. This takes about 5 to 10 minutes; I'll tell you when it's in. · Cancel"), told in the conversation and on its topic when it lands. "Ready by morning" is said only for research the person didn't ask for now: an offer taken up weeks ahead (RA1) and a learn-later question being readied (LL1, LL2, "when you have a free half hour"). Asking for one of those now ("look now", or asking again) runs it at once and says how long, never how it was going to run. **Past the allowance:** Thorough is a locked row, "None left this month · back Dec 1"; a quick look still answers, from general knowledge without reading the web, its card marked "Not from the web", with one `PromptRow` "Look on Dec 1" · "Read the web for this when your looks come back"; only a yes there makes it run on its own on Dec 1 ("I'll look on Tue, Dec 1 and tell you when it's in. · Undo"). **Cancel** stops it and gives back what it held ("Stopped looking into apartments."); an answer that arrives after a Cancel is dropped unread.

**When things go wrong, and other edges (round 3).** Each worded so the person knows what happened and what still works. Drawn where a board is named; the rest are in "Not yet drawn".

| State | What the person sees |
|---|---|
| A reminder arrives for a time already ticked (ticked on a phone with no signal, synced after the push left) | The push as usual; its tap opens Today with the line already "Done 8:05 AM". Nothing apologises; it is the one case the server can't see in time |
| This browser can't show notifications (blocked, or an iPhone outside the Home Screen) | The site's words in the opt-in modal (HF1 Blocked) and on the Reminders sheet (HF4 Off on this browser); the habit is made and kept; its Reminders row reads "On · not on this browser" while another device still takes them, "Off" otherwise |
| Signed out on one phone | That phone gets none (the site takes its token off); other devices still do. Nothing is said in Helm |
| A push tapped when Helm can't open its things | Opening your things, then "Helm can't open your things right now" with Try again (G6); the tick waits for it |
| A push tapped for a habit since paused or stopped on another device | Rare (pausing cancels its reminders); Today opens without the line, and nothing else is said |
| A habit renamed while its name is shown | The next reminder carries the new name; nothing is told |
| Show its name, then the habit is marked health later | The switch stays as set; its warning gains the medicine line |
| More reminders due than a day allows (6) | Later ones fold into the one before; the Habit reminders sheet's foot says "At most 6 a day; ones close together come as one." |
| Delete all my Helm data | Its confirm lists "and every habit reminder" among what goes; pending reminders and every readable name are removed first |
| Two devices answer the same offer or "which comes first?" | The second finds it already answered: the card leaves, and the question leaves Needs you |
| What matters most changed on two devices at once | The view re-sorts live to the last change; each device's Undo line stays on its own screen and undoes only its own move |
| The thing in first place is finished, reached, stopped or deleted | The next moves up; the line where it happened says "Work comes first now. · Change the order" |
| A place said for something finished | "Maya's 30th is finished, so it isn't in the order. Reopen it?" (No · Yes) |
| A place said for a health or care item | "{Name} always keeps your pills, whatever the order, so they aren't placed." Nothing changes |
| Only one thing could be placed | No door and no row; "what matters most?" answers "The wedding is the only thing in progress, so it comes first." |
| A place said offline | The turn waits like any other (O1) and the order changes once it is sorted |
| A guardrail with nothing placed | Its comes-first reason names the nearest deadline instead ("Wedding's guest list is the nearest deadline") |
| A carer stops logging a dose | `useActionModal().confirm` "Stop logging Mom's metoprolol?", "It leaves Today and its reminders stop. Every dose logged stays.", Keep logging · Stop logging; it folds under "Stopped" |
| The phone's time zone differs from Helm's | The one line at Today's top (above), once per difference |
| "I took my pills" when every pill habit today is already ticked | "Already done: morning pills at 8:05 AM." with the ticked line and no Undo; nothing changes |
| "I took my pills" with two that fit, one already ticked | The unticked one is ticked; no Which one? |
| A note said with a check-in on a habit that's paused or formed and off Today | The note is kept with a check-in only if the check-in counts; otherwise "Floss is off Today, so I didn't count it." and the note stays in the conversation |
| The tidy-up would change only punctuation, or the why is under a sentence | No offer; the why is kept word for word |
| The why is changed later | The summary is redone; a tidied version is offered again only if the new why is long or loose |
| Going Once isn't connected, or doesn't share drafts yet | RT2 isn't asked; the plan check's row reads "Things to sell for the move · 1 you mentioned (the couch)" from what the person said, with "Connect Going Once" in its sheet |
| The meeting has no video link or place | Its row isn't a to-settle row; it shows as a fixed event in the plan check's "While you're away" line |
| Where the person will be isn't known | One question first, "Where are you headed?", then the plan check |
| No video good enough for an overview | "Videos" is left out; nothing says none were found |
| The overview's web reading fails | The card stays in Learn later with "Couldn't put the overview together. It'll try again tomorrow."; Today doesn't offer it that day |
| Research can't finish (the web read fails, or nothing fits the brief) | Told on the project's page: "Couldn't find apartments that fit yet. Change what you asked for? · Open the brief" |
| A pattern from words cites a turn later deleted | The quote leaves the pattern; if fewer than 3 remain, the pattern is withdrawn without a word |
| Notice what works is turned off after a "How was it?" was asked | The open question leaves; any answer already given stays as a note |
| A question is spoken and the person says something unrelated | It's a new turn; the question folds to a muted line and waits in Needs you if it had no answer |
| "Hey {Name}" more than 2 minutes after a question | A new turn; the old question stays tappable on screen until the conversation moves on |
| A line in How {Name} sees you loses all its sources | It leaves the page without a word |
| A correction contradicts a later pattern from new words | The correction wins; the new pattern is offered as a question, "Still true that guitar's fine on weekends?" |
| Five traits picked and a sixth tapped | "Up to 5. Drop one first?" under the group; nothing is unpicked for them |
| The phone refuses location | LC1's row reads "Your phone said no · Open your phone's settings"; Helm uses what it's told |
| A time away overlaps another | Both are kept; "where you'll be" uses the one said last for the overlap |
| A Google event has both a video link and a place | Shows both, "Video, or at Milstead & Co."; "nearby" uses the place |
| Research allowance used up  | Thorough is a locked row, "None left this month · back Dec 1"; a quick look still answers from general knowledge, marked "Not from the web", with "Look on Dec 1" (it runs on its own then only after a yes); the cap isn't mentioned anywhere else |
| Research cancelled, and its answer arrives anyway | Nothing shows: the late answer is dropped unread; the topic, if one was started, reads "Stopped looking · Nov 14" |
| A message typed while a spoken question is open | A new turn, answered in writing; the question stays tappable and "Hey {Name}" answers it until 2 minutes after it was asked |
| "Hey {Name}" on a locked phone or an untouched page asks about something private (owner) | Answered without the name ("Your morning medicine is ticked"); names fill in after the first tap |
| A time away in the same time zone as home (Portland) | No time-zone offer; only a stated away in another zone gets one |
| A scheduled action can't run, runs late or is missed | One outcome line on Today on the next open, with its way on (1j); never a push, never amber, never counted in "need you" |
| A who with care habits is forgotten | The confirm names the care habits; they end with their reminders, their logged doses stay and stay private |

## 2. Sitemap


Owner review 2: How {Name} sees you (from Browse and Settings) gains its four groups with sources (SY1) and a Where card in Who you are (LC1); Settings → Your assistant gains How {Name} talks (PT1) and This phone's location (LC2); every who page's Facts tab starts with Where; an event's sheet shows How it meets (MF1).

```
Opening Helm (silent: nothing spoken, the mic closed)
 ├─ Opening your things (Loading, while the data key arrives; a second at most)
 ├─ First open (nothing said yet) → home with one EmptyCard ("Tap Talk and tell {Name} the first thing on your mind"), Talk and Type; no Browse until there is a second view
 ├─ Opened offline, not trusted → home with the offline strip, "Your things open once you're back online", Talk on this phone (O1)
 ├─ Opened offline, trusted → home and every view from this browser, with the offline strip (O2, V6)
 └─ Key refused → "Helm can't open your things right now" + Try again (G6)

Home (the conversation)
 ├─ Header        mark · serif {Name} · Search · Settings
 ├─ At rest       "11:00 AM · Brunch with Maya's parents" → Today · "2 need you" → its first question, asked here · Earlier today · 4 (unfolds today's turns)
 ├─ Top cards     at most 3 (Issues 128, 130): Needs you · the grouping card ("These look like one thing: Moving": Group them · Not related · Not now, one per open) · the day card: morning ("What do you want to get done today?" with "From yesterday") or, in the last 2 hours of the day, "How did today go?" (Done · Still possible tonight · Won't happen today; Tomorrow · Later · Drop it) → folded to one line once answered or dismissed
 ├─ A turn        what you said (+ attachments) → the reply in large type → the card of what {Name} did, each line → its record (swapped in place, Back returns), Undo
 ├─ An answer     records card first, then "Not from your records" → Save to my knowledge · Learn it properly later → Save it
 ├─ Owner review 1: a check-in shows the habit ticked → Which one? (two fit) · How was it? (once, Skip) · Want it tidied up? (a goal's why: Keep mine · Use this) · a plan check ("Can I go…?": Before you go · To settle → each asked in turn: Is this the final list? · Keep it on video? · Do your questions feel ready? · Plan them today)
 └─ Dock          Browse · Talk · Type; while listening: Cancel · Talk · Attach (Take a photo · Choose a photo · Choose a file: PDF or Markdown · Record a voice note)

Browse (a sheet of tiles over home pulled up from the dock's Browse)
 ├─ Search everything → Search
 ├─ Big tiles: Today (wide) · Needs you (tall while questions wait; thin "Nothing waiting" when empty) · People · Projects
 ├─ Small tiles: Coming up (r6e) · Places · Knowledge · Procedures · How {Name} sees you · Habits (r1) (each once it has something)
 ├─ Gap hunt 1: small tile Scheduled · 2 (once one exists) → Scheduled (by next run; Ended · 1) → a scheduled action (Drawer: what it does, next, what it did, Pause for a while · Change when or what it does · End it, Private)
 │   └─ Owner review 5: Coming up → a run (Skip this one · Move this one · Change: Just this one / This and following); Change → How often (presets → Custom… with Ends and the words line)
 └─ Quiet foot row: Connected apps · Settings · Activity
Every content page (round 6e): app bar BackLink (where you came from) · Home (left out when Back is "Home") · ⋯ · its title · the ask bar at its foot ("Ask about today") → the conversation sheet with the chip (✕ → "About anything") · "Back to Today" closes it
Every utility screen (Settings, Connected apps, Activity, forms, Search): BackLink and its title, no ask bar

Today (a view, no longer home)
 ├─ Day strip, all-day events, events, planned and suggested tasks, groups (as round 5)
 ├─ Key dates this week (up to 3 quiet lines, close first, then soonest) → the who's page; "+2 more this week" → Coming up
 ├─ Habits due today (r1): bare lines in the timeline at their time (Water through the day; Pills · morning, now; Pills · evening, 8:00 PM) → tick or + (Undo on the line) · the name → the habit's page
 ├─ On a day a pattern changed (r1): {Name}'s line with Undo → the pattern sheet · a dashed suggestion on a habit it applies to
 ├─ Round 2: the stage word and a bell in a habit line's meta · fading told under the line (Keep them as they were) · "Take it off Today?" under a formed habit · "Pause Stretch…?" under a habit · "Home is back" on the day a pause ends
 ├─ From a habit reminder on the lock screen (r2): Today on that day, the line scrolled into view and tinted (r3: every line the push covers)
 ├─ Round 3: "While you were away… reminders kept coming" once after 3 or more days unopened → Pause them next time · the phone-zone line → Switch to Eastern
 ├─ Owner review 1: "last reminder for now" under a habit after 3 quiet weeks → Keep them coming · a learn-later card in a free slot → the overview (Subview: side by side or steps, Videos · 2 → new tab, Save to my knowledge, Done with it) · "It's felt hard lately?" under a habit on a day it's due
 ├─ Gap hunt 1: one line per scheduled run since you last looked (Done for you · Undo; Ran when you opened; was skipped; didn't run · Reconnect · Skip this one)
Coming up (r6e; from Today and its small tile) → months, 12 ahead → the who's page · Show everyone
 ├─ "2 need you" line → Needs you
 └─ Connect, Reconnect, Couldn't load, another app's things, Two times (as round 5)

Needs you (the old Inbox, no badge)
 ├─ Questions (Which Sam? · Which is true now? · Which day? · Couldn't read this · Couldn't sort this · Sort the rest · Which comes first? (r3, a skipped one)) → asked on home, one at a time
 ├─ Waiting (not counted) → Waiting sheet
 └─ New since you looked → the turn's card on home, scrolled to

People (who: people, pets, organizations)
 ├─ Add from Google Contacts (header action) → Contacts picker (Subview) → Add n people
 ├─ Close · Regular (rows) · Passing · 9 (door)
 └─ A who → its page (Subview; tabs Facts · Tasks · Mentions · Media; Facts opens with Key dates: dates that come back, then Moments (latest 3) → a key date sheet (Edit · Remove · Show on Today) · See all 14 moments → Moments (Subview, by decade, then year) · Add a date; ⋯ More: change name, kind, tier, photo; Forget)
Places → a place (Subview; photo strip; tabs Facts · People · Tasks · Mentions · Media)
Projects
 ├─ Search (from 8) · "Procedures · 4" door · "Finished" door · New project
 ├─ What matters most (r3; a thin door row under the title, "Wedding first") → What matters most (Subview: In order · Not said yet · Can wait · Always kept: health and care; a row → its place sheet: Make it come first · Put it above… (overlay list) · It can wait · Take it out of the order · Open it; drag a placed row by its handle)
 ├─ Each group (Goals, Projects) listed by the order (r3), then by date, then what can wait
 ├─ A guardrail card at the top on the day it is first raised (r2) → Why, and other choices (sheet) · "Paused · 1" and "Set aside · 1" folds
 ├─ Goals (r1; a SectionDivider above Projects, "New goal") → a goal (Subview: the why in the identity, showing up in the evidence line, its place in what matters under it (r3); tabs Tasks (Habits first, "Add a habit") · Milestones · People; ⋯ change, mark reached, set aside, delete)
 └─ A project (Subview; tabs Tasks · Milestones · Procedures · People; People holds key people, a door to the rest, and the project's facts under "What Helm knows": a research offer under the evidence line → "Before I look…" sheet → "What {Name} looked into" → the topic)
Knowledge → Learn later · 2 (door row) → the saved questions → an overview · a grouping suggestion · topics (a researched topic, "📦 Move › Apartments", marked From the web), nested up to 3 levels → a topic (Subview: the document, Show sources → Where this came from, History sheet, its subtopics) · Loose notes → a loose note (sheet)
Procedures → a procedure (Subview: steps, runs, Edit, Start a run) → a run (Subview: ticks, Just this time, Finish this run)
Habits (r1; small tile, A to Z, "Formed · 1" (r2), "Stopped · 1") → a habit (Subview: its stage (r2), this week, notes, earlier weeks, its times, Reminders (r2) → Reminders sheet (r3: Show its name on the lock screen: asks once more when private), Private, part of a goal, its place in what matters (r3) → What matters most, its pattern (from what you said, quotes as evidence), a dashed offer (formed, fewer reminders, pause); ⋯ change, pause, stop (a carer's dose: Stop logging this), delete) → When sheet
 └─ A retired habit (r2): stage, "72 times", earlier weeks; ⋯ Bring it back to Today, delete
How {Name} sees you (Subview) → What I've noticed (r1: a pattern → its sheet with evidence, state and actions; Not used · 1) · Who you are (with your key dates) · How you write · Preferences · Favorites → a fact sheet
A record's Media list → a photo (ImageViewer) · a PDF (new tab) · a voice note (plays in place)

Account page (site, `/account`) → Connected apps (round 5)
Search (Subview) — people, pets, places, tasks, facts, notes, docs, procedures, projects: one list in groups
Settings (Subview)
 ├─ Your assistant → {Name} and tone sheet (with "Say 'Hey {Name}' to try it") · Voice (Hey {Name}, natural voice) · Connected apps
 ├─ Trusted browsers (as round 5)
 ├─ Planning → Your day · Calendars that count
 ├─ Helm on its own (r2) → Habit reminders (sheet: Pause all with Until (r3), each habit → its Reminders sheet) · Notice what works (sheet: the switch; off asks first)
 └─ Your data → Activity · What the AI sees · Delete all my Helm data
```

## 3. Screens


Every board's copy reads `{Name}`. Rows marked "edge" are the unusual states; "r3", "r5" and "r6" mark the round that last changed a row. Round 6 re-draws the older view boards with the dock in place of the tab bar and the Tell pill; their rows below say only what else changed.

| Board | Group | What the picture doesn't show |
|---|---|---|
| Shell: home (r6) | Interactive | Opens on home, silent, every time. At rest: two quiet lines at most (the next fixed thing today, "2 need you" only when > 0) and "Earlier today · 4". Talk walks listening → thinking → speaking on the board (a tap moves it on; thinking moves on by itself). Views open from Browse's tiles and return with Back. |
| Shell: Today (r6e) | Interactive | Under the summary, the week's key dates as quiet lines ("Maya turns 32 · Thu, Nov 12", "Grandpa's birthday · Sat, Nov 14", "Dana Kim's birthday · Tue, Nov 10"), close people first, then soonest, each a door to the who's page; at most 3, then "+2 more this week", which opens Coming up (also a view in the shell). The ask bar reads "Ask about today"; Back reads "Home", so no Home button. The rest as round 5. |
| Shell: Needs you (r6) | Interactive | The old Inbox, reached from "2 need you" or Browse; no badge anywhere. A question tapped here is asked on home. A turn leaves "New since you looked" once its card was seen on home or the person tapped "Mark all seen". |
| Shell: Projects (r6) | Interactive | A big tile in Browse, shown from the first project. A row: the person's emoji (or the name's first letter in a tinted disc), name, the evidence line, chevron. Doors: "Procedures · 4", "Finished · 3". |
| Shell: People (r6) | Interactive | People is a big tile in Browse; Memory's other doors become small tiles (Places, Knowledge). People groups by tier: Close and Regular as rows (photo or initial, name, how they relate, a project role when key), Passing behind a door. "Add from Google Contacts" at the top. Facts live on their who, place or project and on How {Name} sees you; the "{Name} inferred" filter moves to How {Name} sees you and Search. |
| Shell: Settings (r6) | Interactive | From the header's gear on home or the Settings door in the Browse sheet. See S1. |
| G1 Opening your things | First run | Shown only while the data key comes back from the server (sealed content can't be drawn before). The site's `Loading` with the Helm logo, no words for the first 600 ms, then "Opening your things…". After 10 s: "Helm can't reach its server. Your things are safe." with "Try again". |
| G2 First open (r6) | First run | Home with one `EmptyCard` ("Tap Talk and tell {Name} the first thing on your mind. A task, a date, a name: {Name} sorts it."), Talk and Type; no Browse, no glance lines, no calendar row (asked only after the first thing said). Drawn as the shell's First run state. |
| G3 First thing said (r6) | First run | V2 with the AI line once under the words: "{Name} uses an AI service on Helm's server to understand what you say." |
| G4 The reply to your first message (r6d) | First run | The first bubble ("Remind me to call the caterer tomorrow about the vegetarian menu, and Maya's birthday is November 12"), {Name}'s words ("Done. I'll remind you tomorrow at 9, and I've noted Maya's birthday."), the `DidCard` (the task, the key date on Maya, new; "Undo all of this"), then the one-time dashed door "I'm {Name}. You can rename me and choose how I sound." → S2. Talk and Type; Browse joins once there is a second view. |
| G6 Helm can't open your things (r5 wording, edge) | First run | The key callable refused (`failed-precondition`) on a device that holds no key. Header "Helm", no Browse, no search or gear. One card above the field: lock, "Your things can't be opened right now.", "They're safe and nothing was changed. The problem is on our side, not yours.", "Try again" (a Try again that fails again adds "Still can't open them. Your things are safe."). Copy never names the app, so the card speaks for "our side". Until the outbox lands (#885) the dock is hidden and the field waits, saying "Typing works again once your things open"; then Talk and Type work and what is said goes to the device's outbox, as on O1. A trusted browser opens with its own key, so it reaches this only if that key no longer opens anything (then it says the same and drops its trust). A single record that won't open shows "Couldn't open this" in its row, never vanishes. |
| G5 After the first thing said (r6) | First run | Browse appears in the dock with Today and Needs you. Today shows the new task under "Any time today" and, below it, the calendar row. |
| A1 Typing (r6) | Talking | Type opens the compose bar over the dock: Attach, the field (no focus fight: it focuses because the person asked to type), Talk instead, Send (off until there is text). Up to 4,000 characters; from 3,600 a quiet count shows. |
| A2 Typing with @ (r6) | Talking | The shorthand row (`@ Person`, `# Place`, `^ Note`, `! Urgent`; the `^` button is read aloud as "Procedure or note") sits above the keyboard; a tap inserts the character. After `@` the row becomes suggestions from People (up to 3) and "New person 'Da…'". What Helm read is a chip line under the field (Dana Ruiz · Fri, Nov 13 · Urgent), deterministic, before Send. A chip has an ✕ that removes the reading, not the words, and a quiet line under the chips says "Read @Dana as text · Undo" until the next edit or Send. Past 6 chips a "+N more" shows the rest. |
| A3 Listening (r6) | Talking | Drawn as V2. Tap Talk or say "Hey {Name}": a short chime, the button rings, words appear in large type as heard. Ends after 8 seconds of quiet, a tap, or "that's all"; Cancel throws the words away. Mic refused: Talk shows its lock and a tap says how to allow it (`LockedButton`); Type stays. |
| A6 An answer from your records (r3) | Answers | Records answer it in full: a short answer in the chosen tone with its sources (filled or hollow ring, calendar mark, or book mark for a note) and a door to the record; no general part, nothing written, no Undo. |
| A8 Many things (edge, r6) | Talking | A turn that made 12 records shows the first 5 lines on its card and "Show all 12"; the voice says only the count ("Done: 12 things, mostly for the wedding."); Undo still covers all. |
| A9 Your records, then general knowledge (r3) | Answers | Records answer only part of the question ("Who pays for the rehearsal dinner?": Maya said her parents offered). The records card first, then `SectionDivider` "Not from your records", then the general card: globe + "General knowledge", the text in the chosen tone, the caution "From general knowledge, not checked against anything you've told Helm. It may be out of date.", and "Save to my knowledge" (small outline, `BookmarkPlus`). When a note of the person's disagrees, the general card says so in one line ("Your note 'Cake storage' says 5 days."). A question about the person's own things (a date, a person, a task) never falls back to general knowledge; it says "Nothing in Helm says …" and stops. Asked offline, a question waits and is answered in New since you looked. Records that answer fully get no general part (A6); records that hold nothing get the line "Nothing in Helm says …" and then the general part (A11). |
| A10 Daily limit and other holds (r6, edge) | Talking | The held card (muted, an icon per reason, no Undo, chips kept) says why and when, and offers "Make it a task as written" so nobody waits on Helm. Daily limit (`hourglass`): "Saved as written. {Name} has read all it can for today and will sort this after 4:00 PM (the local time the day's allowance comes back)." Ten-minute limit: "Saved as written. That's a lot at once; {Name} will sort it in a few minutes." AI service down (`CloudOff`): "Saved as written. {Name} couldn't reach its AI service and will try again when you next open Helm." Offline: V6. Drawn on home: what you said, then the held card; the voice says it in a few words in the device voice ("Saved. I'll sort it after midnight."). The number (300) is never shown. |
| A11 Nothing in your records (r3, r6d) | Answers | "Nothing in Helm says how long cake keeps." in the records card, then the general part. Save to my knowledge files it (K5 the first time no topic fits, K6 after). With a topic open, the button is "Add to this topic". |
| A12 A health question (r3, edge) | Answers | Carer: "Can Mom take ibuprofen with her metoprolol?" Records part first (Mom takes metoprolol 25 mg, You said Oct 2; nothing about ibuprofen). The general card leads with a bold caution in a muted block with `Stethoscope`: "General information, not medical advice. Check with a doctor or pharmacist before acting on it." Fixed copy, never toned, never names a person (no pronoun guessed for Mom). Shown when the server marks the question as health or medicine. Saving it keeps the caution on the note and on every answer that later cites it. |
| A13 An answer that uses your knowledge (r3, r6d) | Answers | A topic counts as the person's records: "Cake keeps 3 days refrigerated, so the samples are good until Wednesday." Source: book mark "Your topic · 🍰 Kitchen › Cakes · today's version", door to the topic at that part. No general part. |
| B1 Needs you (r6) | Needs you | As the shell view, lived in; no badge. |
| B2 Which Sam? (r6) | Needs you | Asked on home: what you said, then {Name}'s question in the reply card with the candidates as tall choices, each with the one fact that tells them apart ("Work · Design team", "Cousin"), then "Someone new" and "Skip". Spoken too ("Which Sam, Ortiz from work or your cousin Lee?"); answered by voice or a tap. The card then shows the line made, with Undo. From the Needs you view the same question opens as a sheet. |
| B3 Which is true now? | Needs you | Both facts with their sources and dates, as two choices, nothing preselected. The one not picked is kept as "Was $1,800 until Nov 8". **As built (issue 42):** the two statements as tall choices, each with "You said · Nov 3", "You confirmed · Nov 3" or "{Name} inferred · Nov 3" under it, then a quiet "Neither" (the card swaps its choices in place for "What's true now?", one field with Save and Back; Back over typed text asks Keep editing / Discard) and Skip; a question whose facts changed meanwhile closes and says "That changed since."; the newer choice's line ends "· Newest"; answering from a sheet toasts "Kept “Centerpieces are $2,100”" (or "Saved what’s true now") with Undo, and Undo brings the question back; a fact in dispute reads "Disagrees with “Centerpieces are $1,800”" on its sheet, which offers Settle it in place of Confirm (the question swaps in under a chevron Back), and ends its row's muted line with "· Two answers". |
| B4 Turn result (r6) | Needs you | Drawn as V8: what one turn became, on home, in its card. |
| B5 Kept what you changed (edge, r6: also in V8) | Needs you | Undo all after the person moved one of its tasks: "Took back 1 fact and 1 task. Kept 1 task you changed since: Call Dana Ruiz." The kept line stays unstruck. |
| B6 Couldn't read this (edge) | Needs you | {Name} read it and could make nothing of it (a reading that failed three times is "Couldn't sort this"): the words, "Make it a task as written" (primary), "Try again", and the trash. In the conversation the turn says so under its bubble ("Couldn't sort this" when the reading failed, "Read it · nothing to file in it" when it worked and found nothing, "Read again · still nothing to file in it" after a retry) with its own quiet "Try again" beside the words, so a retry never needs a trip to Needs you. When the words seem meant for {Name} but it can't tell what is wanted (garbled speech, a fragment, a word it can't place), it asks one short question in the conversation instead ("What did you mean by 'woodmasses'?"), and the turn never lands here. |
| B7 Inbox empty (edge) | Needs you | One muted line "Nothing new. {Name} files what you tell it here first." No section headers. |
| B8 Held captures, by reason (r3, edge) | Needs you | An edge-state morning, not the lived-in example (badge 3). Needs you: "Couldn't read this", "Too much to file at once · Sort the rest" (a capture past 20 changes; its sheet says how many are filed and makes Sort the rest the footer's primary), "Couldn't sort this" (three failed readings). Below, "Waiting to be read" (hourglass, no dot, not in the badge), each row saying why and when: "Saved on this device · waits for a connection", "Today's limit reached · read after 4:00 PM" (the local time of UTC midnight, when the day's allowance comes back), "A lot at once · sorted in a few minutes". A muted line: "Waiting needs nothing from you: {Name} reads these on its own." A waiting row opens a small sheet: the words, the same reason, "Make it a task as written" and Delete. |
| B11 Questions in every shape (r6) | Needs you | One board, Shape Tweak: Pick one ("Which menu did you and Maya pick?": tall choices, one with the fact that tells it apart, then "Something else" opening "Tell {Name} what you mean"); Pick several ("Which of these need a vegetarian meal?": `Checkbox` rows staged in a card, "None of these" last, "Done · 2"); Yes or no (two equal outlined buttons No · Yes); A few words (a field with a mic, Send); A date or time (three `SuggestionChips`, then a `DateInput` with time, "Pick a day and time"); From your records (`PickOrCreate`: search, the person's projects with their state, "New project" last); A number (− · "18 people" · +, the unit always shown, Save). Every shape ends with Skip on the left; the hint under it says what to say. **As built (issue 36):** `QuestionCard` draws each shape from small parts (`PickOneAnswer`, `PickSeveralAnswer` with tall tick choices, a "None of these" that clears the others and Done reading "Done · 2", `YesNoAnswer`, `FreeTextAnswer`, `DateTimeAnswer`, `PickRecordAnswer` over the person's own people and places, `NumberAnswer`), all in the look of the which-person choices; a turn shows three open questions and then "2 more questions in Needs you", an answered one keeps "You picked …", a Needs you row shows the first prompt with "· 2 more" and "Skipped", and Activity opens with "Asked: … · You picked …". |
| B12 A question answered (r6) | Needs you | Answered by voice: "Sam and Jordan" (one Sam among the options, so Sam Lee); the reply in large type "Got it: Sam Lee and Jordan Hayes."; the card "Noted: Sam Lee and Jordan Hayes need a vegetarian meal" · "You said, answering the question" and "Added to Call the caterer: 2 vegetarian meals" with one "Undo all of this". A muted line under it: "Which menu did you and Maya pick? Skipped. It's waiting in Needs you." Activity keeps "Asked: … · You picked …". |
| B9 Couldn't sort this (r3, edge) | Needs you | After three failed readings. The sentence names the cause: the AI service was down or its reading came back unusable. "Try again" (small, in the body), trash and "Make it a task as written" (primary) in the footer. |
| C1 Today (owner) | Today | As the shell. **As built (issue 39):** a "When you get home" divider (last in today's list) holds the open tasks linked to the home place that have no slot today, with an "I'm home" chip (a house mark) beside its label; after the tap (toast "Home for the next 3 hours" with Undo) the group moves to the top of the day and the chip reads "Till 6:40 PM" with a filled house until it lapses; tapping it asks "Home till 6:40 PM" with "No longer home" (toast "No longer marked home" with Undo). Shown only on today, and only when a home place and such a task exist. |
| C2 Suggestion sheet | Today | Why this slot in one chip ("20 min · fits before the tasting"); Keep (primary), "Another time" (slots for today and tomorrow as pills, then "Pick a day"), "Not today". Not today returns the task to "Any time" with an Undo on the line. A slot whose length is a guess says "about 30 min · estimate". **As built (issue 31):** Today opens on a strip of days (one back, a week ahead) with the day's summary, then one timeline: events, kept tasks as solid cards, suggestions as dashed cards and "Free · 1h 30m" lines between. A suggestion opens this sheet with its reason, Keep, Not today (Undo on the toast) and Another time (up to four pills across today and tomorrow, or "Nothing fits in the next two days"). A past day is C5 with Bring to today; a later day plans when first opened. A task's sheet has Plan it (C9). Settings' Planning group holds Your day (start, end, how full). |
| C3 Today, no calendar | Today | Carer: no times, no free gaps; "Due soon" and "Worth doing today" lists, and one quiet row to connect a calendar that "Not now" folds to Settings for good. |
| C4 Rest day | Today | Nurse, the day after a night shift: events from the calendar, nothing planned, one line "Nothing planned after a night shift. Tell {Name} if you want something today." No suggestions. **As built (issue 32):** the card is `RestDayLine` with a small secondary "Plan my day anyway"; it reads "late night" when no shift calendar is marked, names the day in the sentence ("tomorrow") on a later day, and Due soon and Any time stay. |
| C5 Yesterday | Today | A past day reads as it was: done items ticked (soft), not done unticked under "Not done" with "Bring to today" on its divider, never moved silently. Events are the snapshot taken when the day was planned. Nothing is editable. |
| C6 A full day (edge) | Today | All-day events sit at the top as thin rows; one the person marked busy says "Busy all day". A tentative event says "Tentative". An event set in another zone shows the person's time with the other in its meta ("12:00 PM · 3:00 PM in New York"). A full day: "Your day is full. Nothing suggested." |
| C7 Event sheet | Today | Read only: time, calendar, Tentative, the zone note; "What Helm knows" facts linked to the event; its procedure run (suggested or kept), its Media; for an all-day event an `AppToggle` "Keeps me busy all day". Footer: "Open in Google Calendar" (secondary, leaves Helm in a new tab). **As built (issue 32):** a calendar event's sheet opens with its day and time ("Today · 3:00 to 4:00 PM", and Tentative), an all-day one has the switch row that writes at once with its failure on the row, and the one "Open in Google Calendar" link is the source block's. |
| C8 Calendar trouble (edge, r3: drawn as two boards) | Today | Two cases. Stopped sharing (Google revoked it): Today drops to the no-calendar version with a row "Google Calendar stopped sharing your events · Reconnect". Couldn't load (anything else): kept slots stay, events are missing, and a muted line says "Couldn't load your calendar. Your plan is below; check it against your calendar." Never shown as an empty day; the planner suggests nothing until events load ("No suggestions until your calendar loads.") and the line carries "Try again". Offline is O2's line instead. Stopped sharing keeps planned slots, moves suggestions to "Worth doing today" untimed, and the summary reads "No calendar · 1 planned". **As built (issue 32):** stopped sharing is one row per stopped account in Today's top strip with its own Reconnect button; couldn't load is `CalendarFailedLine` on every day (Try again asks the platform to read again), with suggested slots hidden and no plan made until the read works. Stopped sharing also holds planning ("No suggestions until your calendar is reconnected.") rather than moving suggestions untimed, since a plan that ignores events it can't read could land on one. A kept slot a new event covers reads "Overlaps Lunch with Sam" with Another time. |
| C9 Plan it result (edge) | Today | From a task's "Plan it": the sheet swaps to the slot {Name} found ("Today 5:50 to 6:20 PM · fits after the guest list"), Keep / Another time. No slot: "No room today or tomorrow. Pick a day" with the day pills. |
| D1 Projects | Projects | As the shell tab. **As built (issue 45):** Projects joins Browse beside People once the first project exists; rows show the emoji, name and the evidence line, then a "Finished · n" fold; header add "New project". |
| D2 Wedding · Tasks | Projects | Identity header (emoji, serif name, objective, target date), the evidence line, pill tabs. Tasks: overdue first, then by deadline, then undated; 6 then "Show all 14". Latent tasks sit last under "Not yet (2)", folded. **As built (issues 45 and 46):** tabs are Tasks, Milestones and People; the "Not yet" fold arrives later. Done tasks of the project that are not cleared sit in a fold at the bottom, "Done · 3", ticked (a tick reopens and says "Order the invitations is open again"), each row reading "Done Oct 3" and opening the task's sheet, with "Clear 3 done tasks" on the fold's header line while it is open; Clear toasts "Cleared 3 done tasks" with Undo and says "There was nothing left to clear." when another device got there first. |
| D3 Wedding · Milestones | Projects | Dated rows, reached ones ticked and dimmed at the top in one fold "Reached · 2". The next one carries "in 9 days". Header add "Add milestone". **As built (issue 46):** open rows by date (undated last, then in the order made), each with the same round tick as a task and a line like "Mar 3 · in 9 days" for the next open dated one, "Mar 3 · 4 days ago" for one that passed, a bare date for the rest and "No date"; reached ones fold below as "Reached · 2". A row opens a sheet (title, optional date, Cancel and Save, Delete with a confirm, Keep editing / Discard); a tick and every change toast with Undo. A finished project shows its milestones with no add, tick or sheet. The tab carries no heading of its own: "Add milestone" sits right-aligned above the rows. |
| D4 Wedding · Procedures (r6) | Projects | Procedures used for the project, each with its last run ("Wedding vendor call · run 2 of 3"); a suggested one is the dashed row ("Vendor tasting · Suggested procedure · 6 steps") with Keep / Not now in its sheet. |
| D5 Wedding · People (r6) | Projects | Key people as rows (photo or initial, name, "key person" and how they relate); one door "Involved · 6 · mentioned · 9"; then `SectionDivider` "What Helm knows" and the project's facts with their rings; a pending contradiction shows as "Two answers · Which is true now?". A who's role is changed from its row's sheet (key person, involved, mentioned). **As built (issue 45):** Key and involved rows, a fold "Also mentioned · n", "Add someone" (an existing person or "Someone new"); no facts section yet. Every change has Undo. |
| D6 Project ⋯ More | Projects | Drawer: "Change name, objective or date", "Mark finished" (asks nothing; Undo on the toast; open tasks stay open and say so), "Delete project" (confirm: "Its 14 tasks stay, without a project"). **As built (issue 45):** Delete confirms with the task count; its Undo restores the project and re-links the same tasks. |
| D7 New project | Projects | `QuestionFlow`: Name and emoji → "What does done look like?" (one sentence) → "Is there a date?" (Skip) → review. Creates with Undo on the project page's first line. **As built (issue 45):** The review step creates with an Undo toast and opens the project; a name already used by an active project opens that one. |

Call the florist on Friday at 10 AM." Tapping a voice saves it and plays its sample, and changing the speed saves it and plays the chosen voice at the new speed; a change made while a sample plays restarts it with the new choice, and switching tabs leaves it playing. Locked states show one notice at the top with its fix and no lock on any row: device-only ("The cloud voice is off, so this device reads replies in its own voice." with "Use the cloud voice", which turns the switch off) and offline ("You're offline. Samples play once you're back."). Failures keep the screen as it was: "Couldn't save that voice. It stays as it was." and "The sample didn't play." While locked, a tap still saves the voice but plays nothing, and the Play a sample button is not drawn. The download is offered only after a check that the phone can run it; it waits for Wi-Fi unless the person says otherwise. |

A word matches a title or the body, in any order, accents and case ignored; a title match comes first. Tasks list open ones, then done ones with a filled check and "Done Nov 3"; Dates match "birthday", a custom label or a month ("June"), moments included; a note row shows its topic or "Loose note"; a synced row shows its source's logo and name. A person, place, project, topic or procedure opens its page; a date opens its person; a fact opens its person, place or project, else How {Name} sees you; a task, note or synced copy opens its sheet over Search; a photo opens the record it is on, else its viewer. Empty field: "Find anything you have told {Name}: people, tasks, dates, facts, notes and more." Nothing found: `Nothing matches "x". Searched your people, tasks, dates, facts, notes, topics, projects, procedures, places, photos and connected apps. Mail and Drive are searched only when you ask {Name}.` While done tasks or older dates are still being read a small line says so ("Still looking through done tasks…"). The typed words stay in the address, so Back from a result lands on the same list, and Back there says "Search". |

- **Owner review 4:** the Private row on a who's, note's, topic's, procedure's, goal's and project's page; "Its name is hidden on your lock screen again." under the row; a copied list with "2 private things left out · Include them" and after Include them; a later turn marking a task private ("Marked private · about health"); "It's not private" asked in a turn; the lock in Search, What matters most and Coming up, and at 320 on a long name; Y4's "Hey {Name}" over Settings ("About anything"); LC2's City only drawer after a City only offer.

- Owner review 1: "Already done: morning pills" (everything already ticked); a check-in note on a habit off Today; a health note's "Has that happened before?"; the habit page's "Show all" notes; "Change the why" and a changed why's new summary; GO5's review step with the tidy-up offer; LN7 on Today under the habit's line, and its third no; "Forget these words" done; Notice what works off with an open "How was it?"; HF10 for a carer's dose; RT1 with "Where are you headed?" first; RT1 without Going Once connected; "Plan them today" done on Today; RT2's "That's everything" and "Finish it in Going Once"; RT3's "Move it" day pick and the note's sheet; a skipped to-settle question in Needs you; RT4 after Sunday; the Learn later list at 8+ with search, and an item's ⋯ (Remove); LL2's Not now; LL3's key-points form, an overview with no videos, and its failure line; RA1's Not now; RA2 said in a turn, one missing answer at a time; the researched topic itself ("📦 Move › Apartments", the brief at its top, From the web parts) and research that found nothing; Activity rows for a plan check and a research run; the desktop (`lg`) of row 19.

- Round 3 (what matters most, the lock screen, away): Clear the order and its yes or no; "Take it out of the order" done; a drag in progress (the lifted row and its gap); four or more said equal and "Which comes next?"; the skipped "Which comes first?" opened from Needs you; "Work comes first now · Change the order" on a finished project; a name that fits two things; "Make Spanish a goal?" before placing it; a place said for a health item; the Always kept list opened; Projects with two sharing first ("Wedding and Work first"); the carer's "Stop logging" confirm; a folded push naming one habit and counting the rest ("💊 Pills and 1 more evening habit"); the Reminders row "On · not on this browser"; Delete all's confirm with reminders listed; the desktop (`lg`) of row 18, where What matters most sits beside Projects in the left-panel layout.

- Round 2 (forming, guardrails): a habit with reminders on two windows folded into one push with another habit; Building again told on Today (the first push after it is on HF2's note only); "Turn them back on" for a formed habit; a health habit's "Only if I'm late" answered; a carer's reminders for Mom; the browser's own permission prompt; Bring it back to Today; the Formed fold opened; a guardrail on a habit's page (only on the nurse's Today); Set aside's return line on Nov 18 and "Home is back"; "Find a later day" from GR1's task; "Pause until a day you pick" with its date; a third no on either offer; Pause all habit reminders switched on; Noticing off on What I've noticed; the desktop (`lg`) of rows 16 and 17.

- Round 1 (habits, goals, noticing): the Habits list and its tile; "Stopped · 1"; a counted habit past its number ("7 today"); a habit with three windows; a carer's habit page for Mom (drawn only on Today); the delete and stop confirms; a goal's People tab, ⋯ More, "Mark reached" and "Set it aside" with its fold; a goal with no habit ("No habit yet"); GO5's other steps; "Show all 7" patterns and the "Not used" list opened; Activity rows for a pattern change and its Undo; a second suggestion waiting behind the first; a pattern on a goal's task rather than a habit; the desktop (`lg`) of every round-1 board. Every board is drawn light; dark is the shell's Theme Tweak.

- Round 6: an organization's page (the who page with a building mark); a pet with no photo; the Procedures view list and the procedure editor (D11); Start a run's "What is it for?" sheet; a task's steps opened in place; the compose bar with the attach menu open; a voice note sent (its waveform thumb) and played on a record; a kept Markdown file as a doc; Earlier today unfolded; "Tell me more" reading on; the voice limit line; the wake phrase switched off; the rename field's "Say "Hey {Name}" to try it" result; the Contacts picker's account pills and its Contacts permission refused; a who's tier and role sheets; the Needs you view's question sheet; desktop (`lg`), where home keeps the conversation in a centred column and Browse becomes a left rail of the same tiles beside it; the dark theme for rows 11 and 12; the Add a date form and its kind picker; a who with no key dates (one "Add a key date" row); Search opened from Browse; Browse in first days (only Today and Needs you, small tiles appearing as views fill) and with "More · 3"; a question's "Something else" field open, "Pick at least one, or None of these", a turn with more than 3 questions, and a skipped question opened from Needs you.

- Round 6e: the ask bar on a place, project, task, procedure, Needs you, People and Projects lists, Knowledge, How {Name} sees you and an answer's source (drawn on Grandpa, Miso, Car care, Today, Moments and Coming up); a long name cut by the ellipsis; Coming up empty, with "Show everyone" on, and with a month past 20; a moment's Edit form; Moments empty; the who page empty (one "Add a key date" row); a who with more than 3 dates that come back.
- Round 6d: "Show sources" switched on across a whole document; a version opened from History with "Restore this version"; "Read them now" opening the waiting notes; "Topics go three levels deep…"; the delete confirm; "Already in Washing"; "Two of your notes differ: 3 days or 5" in a document; "Write" (the person editing the document by hand); Change topic's picker; X2 at 320px.

- Round 5: the account page's Connected apps section; the Google account's own page (Calendar, Gmail, Drive, healthy); the app-can't-be-reached and lost-access versions of L5; "Not allowed on Google's screen" after an unticked box; the first copy arriving ("Updating…"); turning a category off with its Undo line; a kept copy after Disconnect on Today; the Today line for an app that hasn't updated in a day; the dark theme for row 10.

- Milestone sheet; "Mark all seen" state; the Your day sheet (time zone, day hours, how full a day in one sheet) and the Google Calendar Subview from Settings (F3 with "Connected as" and Disconnect).
- The waiting capture's sheet (B8 row → words, reason, Make it a task as written, Delete); a topic's ⋯ More and delete confirm; the empty Knowledge view; the topic picker opened from Change topic; the note delete confirm; a general card that names a disagreeing note; a question asked offline waiting for its answer; Trust this browser switched on (its "on" words) and its failure line; the Remove confirm.
- Which day? (two dates in one capture).
- Merging two people {Name} made separately (Next Steps).
- Desktop (`lg`): Needs you and Projects as list beside detail.
- Next Steps surfaces (other documents, calendar write proposals with their Proposed → Running → Done / Failed / Needs checking states). The proposal card is drawn only when the first action that leaves Helm is planned, so it is never a disabled stub in the MVP.

## 5. Where the screen set stops


- **Notes on a habit**: the latest two on its page, then "Show all", a list by day, newest first, in pages of 20. **Learn later**: one list in Knowledge, newest first, search from 8, no cap (a saved question is a line of text); at most one brought back a day. **A plan check**: at most 5 rows before the card folds the rest behind "And 3 more" (a long weekend's list is a sign the verdict should be a guardrail, not a longer list). **Videos**: at most 2 per overview, never a list.

- **What matters most** (round 3): every running project, goal and goal-less habit (about 30 habits at the cap plus projects and goals; drawn at 50 on IM6), one list with search from 8 and no paging; the Always kept list holds health and care items (at most the 30-habit cap plus care projects). "Put it above…" lists In order only.

- **Scheduled**: at most 50 actions, one list by next run, search from 8, ended ones folded (30 at a time); a sheet shows its last three outcomes, then "See all in Activity"; Today shows three outcome lines at most, then "N more in Activity".
- **Browse** has four big tiles, fixed; small tiles two to a row, up to 8, then the last is "More · 3" opening the rest in place.
- **Habits** (round 1): Today draws every habit due today, 8 lines at most, then "3 more habits today" opening the Habits list; the list is A to Z with search from 8 and stopped ones folded. A habit's page shows this week and four earlier weeks, then "Show earlier", 12 weeks at a time back to its start (a habit kept for years is 150 weeks). A goal's Habits group lists every habit; its Tasks are a project's. Round 2: the Formed and Stopped folds list 30 at a time; a retired habit's page shows four earlier weeks then "Show earlier" as before; Settings' Habit reminders sheet lists every habit with reminders (30 at most, the habit cap); a counted habit keeps 3 reminder times.
- **What I've noticed** (round 1) shows 3, then "Show all 7"; the full list folds stopped and no-longer-true patterns behind "Not used · N", 30 at a time. A pattern's evidence lists the occurrences that made it (at most 8) and, when asked again, only those since the decline.
- **Key dates** (round 6e, for 2,000 whos and 20,000 dates): a who page lists every date that comes back (a few) and the latest 3 moments, then "See all 14 moments"; Moments opens the three newest decades and folds the rest to counts, loading 30 at a time. Today shows 3 lines, then "+2 more this week". Coming up reads 12 months ahead, a month at a time, 20 per month then "Show 14 more in March"; passing people only behind "Show everyone".
- **Home** shows the latest turn, Earlier today folded (today's turns only, 20 then "Show earlier"), and nothing older; Activity holds every turn.
- **People** groups Close, Regular and a Passing door; search from 8; Passing lists 30 at a time. A who's Media lists 12 then "Show all".
- **Procedures** lists A to Z with search from 8; a procedure past 50 steps folds its groups; a procedure's runs list the last 5 then "Show all runs".
- **How {Name} sees you** shows 5 per group, then "Show all 23".
- **Needs you** holds only what needs the person and what is unseen, so it stays short; Activity holds everything, newest first, 30 at a time under `LazyMount`.
- **Today** shows one day; the strip reaches 7 days ahead and 1 back. Older days open from a task's history only.
- **Projects** groups Active and Finished; search from 8. A project's Tasks tab shows 6 then "Show all"; at 50 it gets a search and a group per milestone-free / overdue / dated.
- **A who's facts** load 30 at a time; a person with 50 facts shows 10 and "Show all 50"; Mentions 10 then "Show all". Versions (procedures) list 10 then "Show older".
- **Search** shows 3 per group, then the group in full on "Show all".
- **Knowledge** lists topics as a tree, A to Z at each level, three levels at most; past 30 top-level topics the tree folds subtopics behind their parent. Search is always there. A topic's document has no row cap (it is prose); a parent lists every subtopic. History lists 10 versions then "Show older". Loose notes list 30 at a time, newest first. A part's sources list every source.
- **Connected apps** (round 5) is one list of every Google account and every site app the person can open, a dozen rows today; past 20 it gets a search. Google accounts have no cap in the UI (tech may set one). A connection's categories are the app's own short list.
- **Other trusted browsers** has no cap (round 5, the site standard); its summary merges alike names and shows three, then "N more", and the sheet lists every one.

## 6. Reusable components


**New in owner review 5.** **Shared from the first use** (Waypoint's events, Runway's items, Nine Lives' visits and every reminder are likely to want it; no app's words in any part): `RepeatPicker` (`src/components`: the field with its words line, the presets worded from a first date, Custom with every N, week days, a month's day or nth or last weekday, a year's date, and Ends never / on / after; it swaps its views inside the host sheet with `BackLink` and `useBackStep`, never a popover or a second sheet; props: the rule, the first date, an optional time, caps such as the smallest unit and the most times) with `RepeatSummary` (the words line alone), and `describeRepeat` / `nextRepeats` in `@/utils/repeatUtils` (the words for a rule and its next dates, one function every surface calls). Its Pattern index line, for the docs PR: "a repeat (Does not repeat, every day, weekday, week, month or year, Custom with every N, the days, a month's day or its nth or last weekday, Ends never, on a date or after N times) is `RepeatPicker`, its presets worded from the first date like a calendar's, its rule in words with the next three dates from `describeRepeat` (`@/utils/repeatUtils`), swapped in place inside the sheet; Helm's scheduled actions are the reference." Habits keep HB5's own When (a number a week is not a repeat). The sound-alike name fix extends `@/utils/optionMatchUtils` (tech's), and the falling chime is a second cue in the speech lib's listening sound. **App-scoped:** `RunSheet` (RP4's in-place run view), `NamesSheet` (Also called and Also heard as), `FamilyRow` with its `FormSheet`, `HeardWord` (the dotted-underlined word and its compact `Drawer`), `MedicineQuestion` (MC1). Reused: `Stepper`, `SegmentedControl`, `DateInput`, `ActionRow`, `OptionGroup`, `AssistantLine` (its quiet form for "Okay, left it."), `ListeningStrip`, `Drawer`, `SkipQuestionButton`.

**New in gap hunt 1, app-scoped.** `ScheduledList` and `ScheduledActionSheet` (SA1, SA2, on `Drawer`, `ActionRow`, `FormSheet`), `ScheduledOutcomeLine` (SA3: done with Undo, ran late, skipped, didn't run with its way on; the same on Today and in Activity), `MedicineCareRow` (a habit's immediate switch above `PrivateRow`, sharing its reason line and confirm shape). `AbilityOfferRow` gains an optional second action line under a hairline (SA4), still with no app's words in it.

**New in owner review 4, app-scoped.** `PrivateRow` (the switch, its subtitle, the reason line with Change and its confirm; one component on every record's sheet or page, so the words never drift), `PrivateMark` (the 12px lock after a name, in every list and `DidCard` row), `AbilityOfferRow` (LC2: an ability the assistant offers under an answer: icon, label with the level, a keeps line, Not now · Turn on, and the phone-said-no line in Turn on's place; built with no location words in it, the ability's icon, words and the request made inside the tap passed in, since it will offer reminders and the microphone too). Reused: `AppToggle`, `useActionModal().confirm`, `ConversationSheet` with `SpokenText` and the speaking status and Mute (Y4), `HabitReminderSheet` (the private line and the confirm, HF8).

**New in owner review 2, app-scoped.** `ListeningStrip` (under a question's choices: the phrases to say, the draining 8-second bar, its ended state), `NotedLine` (AN1), `GoingWellCard` (WS1, on the pattern card's parts in `accent`), `TraitPicker` with `ReplySample` (PT1, on `MultiPillGroup`, `NotchedSlider`, `InlineNotice`), `WhereCard` (LC1), `MeetingFormatRow` (MF1). The listening window is general: if a second app asks back by voice, `ListeningStrip` moves to `src/components` without Helm's words.

**New in owner review 1, app-scoped.** `CheckInRow` (a `DidCard` row drawn as the habit line, ticked or counted; HB2), `WhichHabitQuestion` (HB2, on the pick-one shape with a tick per choice), `CheckInNoteRow` and `HowWasItQuestion` (HB6, on `QuestionCard`'s words shape with `SuggestionChips`), `HabitNotes` (the page's Notes list), `WhySheet` (GO6: the shown why, the original behind "See what I said", "In short"), `TidyOffer` (GO6's reply card with the quote block), `ChoiceSuggestionCard` (`SuggestedCard` holding outcome rows; LN7), `PlanCheckCard` (RT1, RT4: groups with counts, tick and to-settle rows, one button), `LearnLaterRow` and `LearnOverview` (LL3; forms side by side, steps, key points), `ResearchBriefSheet` (RA2, a `FormSheet` whose fields carry their source). **New in round 3, app-scoped.** `PriorityList` (IM1: the three groups, numbered In order rows with a drag handle, search from 8), `PlaceSheet` (IM4: the `ActionRow`s and the "Put it above…" overlay), `PlaceRow` (IM5: the quiet row on a project's or goal's page, and a habit's summary row), `WhichFirstQuestion` (IM3, built on the existing pick-one question shape, so it can wait in Needs you), `AwayLine` (HF9, an `AssistantLine` with its one action). A press-and-drag reorder is not shared yet: check `src/components` and Dreamer UI first, and if none exists and a second app is likely to want one (Runway's Priorities is the obvious one), build it shared from this first use as `ReorderList` with no app's words, per CLAUDE.md "Build it shared from the first use" (asked of tech).

**Existing:** `PhotoPicker` + `useImageUpload` (a who's photo), `PhotoStrip` and `ImageViewer` (a place's photos, media), `NaturalVoiceOption`, `readAloud` and `toSpokenText` (spoken replies), `useSpeechCommands` and `readWakePhrase` (listening, "Hey {Name}", "that's all"), `Drawer`, `FormSheet`, `ModalFooterActions`, `QuestionFlow`, `Subview`, `SectionHeader`, `SectionDivider`, `SearchInput`, `PillGroup` (`isThin` for filters), `Tabs variant='pills'`, `EmptyCard`, `TickMark`, `UndoSnackbar`, `DetailSheet`, `SummaryRow`, `ShowMoreButton`, `RevealChip`, `BackLink`, `HelpTip`, `InlineNotice`, `LabelChip`, `ActionRow`, `SettingsGroup`/`SettingsRow`, `AppToggle`, `UserAvatar`, `AvatarStack`, `IconBadge`, `CopyTextButton`, `ExternalLinkText`, `LockedButton`, `LazyMount`, `Loading`, `AppLogo`, `EmojiField`, `NotchedSlider` (importance, how full a day), `TimezoneSelect`, `useDiscardGuard`, `useFormDraft` (an unsent capture survives the phone closing the app), `useSpeechCommands`, `useActionModal().confirm`.

The bottom tab bar no longer moves to shared for Helm: Helm has none (round 6).

**New in round 6, app-scoped:** `ConversationHome` (glance lines, Earlier today, the turn), `TalkDock` (Browse or nothing · Talk · Type, its four states and lock), `ComposeBar`, `TurnCard` (bubbles with thumbnails, the words, then `DidCard`: rows with icons and one Undo; r6d), `SpokenText` (words turning ink as said), `AskBar` (r6e: the shell's one ask strip at a content page's foot, label from the page's context, mic and keyboard; replaces `ContextBar`), `ContextChip` ("About Grandpa" with ✕ → "About anything"), `ConversationSheet` (the conversation over a page, "Back to …" on top), `PageBar` (r6e: Back, Home unless Back is Home, ⋯), `BrowseSheet` (search, the bento, the foot row) with `BrowseTile` (`size`: wide, tall, big, thin, small), `QuestionCard` (the question, its shape's control, Skip; shapes `pickOne`, `pickSeveral`, `yesNo`, `words`, `dateTime`, `record`, `number`, built on `PillGroup`/choice rows, `Checkbox`, `DictationField`, `SuggestionChips` + `DateInput`, `PickOrCreate`, a stepper), `KeyDateList` (r6e: dates that come back, the Moments divider, the latest 3) and its sheet, `KeyDateLine` (Today's quiet line), `MomentsList` (decades, years, folded decades), `ComingUpList` (months, 20 then Show more), `AttachMenu`, `MediaThumb` and `MediaList`, `WhoAvatar` (photo, initial, paw or building), `TierRow` and `RoleRow`, `TopicDoc` (path, title, the document, subtopic links; r6d), `SourcesSheet` ("Where this came from"), `TopicHistory`, `DocStatusLine` (Bringing in / tomorrow / Try again), `GroupingCard` (the dashed suggestion), `WhereQuestion` (K5, a `QuestionCard` pick-one with an editable "New topic"), `ProcedureSteps` and `RunHeader`.

## 7. User journeys


- **Keep something private:** Today → Buy the rings → its sheet → Private on → the row on Today shows the lock (→ or say "keep the rings private" → "Buy the rings is private now", Undo). A thing {Name} marked: Pills's page → "Marked private because it's about health · Change" → "Make this not private?" → Keep it private.
- **Show a private thing's name on the lock screen:** Pills's page → Reminders → "Show its name on the lock screen" → "Show Pills's name on your lock screen?" → Show its name → Save → the next reminder reads "💊 Pills · evening".
- **Ask a page out loud:** Grandpa's page → the ask bar's mic (or "Hey {Name}") → "When's his birthday?" → answered aloud, Mute under the line → Back to Grandpa.
- **Let it use where you are, when it helps:** "Is there a good coffee place near me?" → answered from your trip, with "Use this phone's location · Precise" → Turn on → the phone asks → Allow → answered again from where you are (→ Not now: not offered again for 4 weeks unless you ask).
- **Answer without your hands:** Talk → "I took my pills" → "Pills or Vitamin D?" spoken, listening → "both" → both ticked, Undo (→ silence for 8 seconds → "Say “Hey {Name}” to answer" → "Hey {Name}, both").
- **Correct what {Name} believes:** Browse → How {Name} sees you → "Guitar has felt hard on weeknights" → its three quotes → That's not quite right → "only weeknights after work; weekends are fine" → the line changes, Undo (→ or say "no, guitar's fine on weekends" anywhere).
- **Shape how it talks:** Settings → Your assistant → How {Name} talks → tick Blunt with Gentle on → the clash notice → Keep both → the sample changes (→ or say "be more blunt with me").
- **Tell it where you'll be:** "I'm in Leavenworth Sunday to Tuesday" → "Noted: you're in Leavenworth Sun to Tue · Change" → "nearby" in RT3 (this phone's location is never asked up front; LC2 offers it under an answer that needs it).

- **Tick a habit by saying it:** Talk → "I took my pills" → "Done: morning pills." with the habit line ticked in the card (→ Undo) (→ two fit → "Which one?" → Pills / Vitamin D / Both → ticked) (→ "took my pills, felt a bit dizzy after" → ticked, the note kept).
- **Say how it went:** Talk → "practised guitar for half an hour" → ticked → "How was it?" → Hard / a few words → "Kept that with tonight's practice." (→ Skip → nothing more today) → the habit's page → Notes.
- **Keep the why in your words:** Talk → "I want to get back into piano" → "Why does it matter to you?" → a long spoken answer → "Kept that as the why, word for word." → "Want it tidied up?" → Use this → the goal shows the tidied why (→ Keep mine) → tap the why → "Why it matters" → See what I said → the original (→ Show mine instead).
- **Change a habit that's felt hard:** open Helm the morning after a third "too tired" → the habit's page → "Guitar practice has felt hard lately. Change something?" → Make it smaller → "Practice is 10 minutes now. · Undo" (→ Try another time → When sheet with suggested times → Save) (→ Try something new → three ideas → pick one) (→ Not now → not before Dec 11) (→ Why, and other choices → the quotes → Forget these words).
- **Get the last reminder:** three weeks, nothing ticked → the lock screen "Your evening habit · last reminder for now" → tap → Today, the line tinted → Keep them coming (→ tick it → reminders carry on) (→ ignore it → next open: "Stretch's reminders stopped on Nov 29 · Turn them back on").

## 8. Form fields


Owner review 4 adds **Private** (an immediate `AppToggle`, off unless {Name} marked it for health or care) to the later set of every row below that is a record (task, habit, goal, project, note, topic, fact, who, procedure), entered from its sheet or page, and makes the lock-screen name switch part of every Reminders sheet, off by default.

Round 3 adds one row (what matters most, below the habit rows) and, on the Habit row, the lock-screen name switch. Owner review 1 adds the check-in note, the goal's why in three parts, a learn-later item and a research brief.

Most records are made by talking to {Name}, so their create form is a sentence. These are the hand-made and edit paths.

| Entity | Initial set | Later set | Later entry point | Grouping | Fixed choices |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Turn (what you said) | Words (spoken or typed); attachments (photo, PDF, Markdown, voice note; up to 5) | None | None | None | Attachment kinds closed: photo, PDF, `.md`, voice note (video later) |
| Task | Title (from the capture or typed) | When: a deadline (date, or date and time) · Not until (a date, or after another task) · Context · Place (a place Helm knows, "when I get home") · Person (a who Helm knows) · Project · Importance · Urgent (on/off, `!`) · Length · Energy · Waits on (a task or a person) · Notes · Steps | Catch-all: the task sheet's summary rows (When, Who, Project, Steps on the front; the rest under "More details"). Contextual: "Another time" on a suggestion writes the plan slot only, never the deadline. | Two summary groups, no Disclosure needed | Context: at home, out, at a computer, on the phone (closed). Energy: low, medium, high (closed). Importance: Must, Should, Could on a `NotchedSlider` (closed). Length: 15m, 30m, 1h, 2h pills plus "Other" typed minutes. |
